Plan code:
```python
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _fft_len(m): n=1; while n<m: n<<=1; return n

def _conv(x): # full linear conv x with itself only? For general maybe use np.convolve small? Use FFT.
   N=...
   F=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
   y=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*len(x)-1]
   y[y<0] = 0? small negative roundoff.
   return y

def _load(p,u,Nload, F? ):
   L=len(u); n=len(p)
   pr=p[::-1]
   t=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(pr,Nload)*np.fft.rfft(u,Nload), Nload)[n-1:n-1+n]
   return 2*t
```

For scaled q with max1, q sum Q. The active constraints correspond to coefficients of Q(z)^2 =1, and inactive <1. We want maximize Q(1). This is similar to the problem of maximizing P(1) with P^2 ≤ U where U is some sequence of 0/1 indicating active constraints. If we assume active set is a contiguous block of length L (say from s to s+L-1), and maybe endpoints inactive, then P(z)^2 has coefficients 1 on that block. To maximize P(1), we can choose P as the square root of a polynomial with coefficients 1 on active block and unknown on inactive (≤1). The maximal P likely has as many coefficients active as possible and equal 1. The tail-active set active block length n at the end. Maybe another block length 2n-1 but impossible. What active blocks yield better P(1)? We can parameterize P(z)^2 = R(z) where R_k ≤1, R active =1, and P has nonnegative coeffs. ```
def make_smooth_Objective(n, p0, alpha, C):
    L=2*n-1
    Nconv=next_pow2(L+1?)
    Nload=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    def fun_theta(theta):
        theta = np.asarray(theta)
        # fix theta0? theta length n-1. For stability, subtract max from all including 0.
        alltheta = np.empty(n); alltheta[0]=0; alltheta[1:]=theta
        mtheta = alltheta.max(); alltheta -= mtheta
        exp = np.exp(alltheta)
        p = exp/exp.sum()
        y = conv_self(p,Nconv)
        # smooth max average LSE
        M = y.max()
        e = np.exp((y-M)/alpha)
        Z = e.sum()
        # objective C*(M + alpha*(log(Z)-log(L)))
        # guard Z>0; M finite.
        return C*(M + alpha*(np.log(Z) - np.log(L)))
    def funjac(theta):
        ... p,y,M,e,u,Z
        m = C*(M + alpha*(np.log(Z)-np.log(L)))
        load = load_fft(p,u,Nload)  # derivative of m_y
        l = C*load
        dot = np.dot(p,l)
        grad = p*(l-dot)
        grad = grad[1:]  # reduce fixed 0
        return m, grad
```

Let's design active-set mirror loop in more detail:
```
def active_step(p, eta, delta_frac):
    y=conv(p)
    M=y.max()
    thresh=M - delta_frac*M
    act = y>=thresh
    if act.sum()==0: act[y==M]
    u=np.zeros(L); u[act]=1/act.sum()
    load=load_fft(p,u)
    # normalize load to zero mean and std 1
    l=load - load.mean()
    s=np.sqrt(np.mean(l*l))+1e-12
    l/=s
    # direction: decrease high load -> log p -= eta*l (since l high positive)
    p_new = p * np.exp(-eta*l)
    p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-300); p_new/=p_new.sum()
    return p_new, M
```

Detailed code structure:

```python
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

def _next_pow2(m):
    n=1
    while n<m:
        n <<=1
    return n

def _softmax_log(theta, fixed_first=True):
    if fixed_first:
       n=len(theta)+1
       allnp.empty(n); all[0]=0; all[1:]=theta
    else: all=theta
    all = all - all.max()
    all = np.clip(all, -745, 745) # after shift max 0, min -745
    e=np.exp(all)
    return e/e.sum()

def _make_convolver(n):
   L=2*n-1
   N=_next_pow2(L)
   def conv_self(p):
       F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       y=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
       # remove tiny negative
       y = np.where(y<0, 0.0, y) # but negative could indicate underflow? small.
       return y
   return conv_self
```
For load, need N2. We could compute `p_rev` each time. Could precompute a permutation? No.

```
def _make_loader(n):
   N2=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   def load(p,u):
       pr=np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])
       Fpr=np.fft.rfft(pr,N2)
       Fu=np.fft.rfft(u,N2)
       c=np.fft.irfft(Fpr*Fu,N2)
       return 2*c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Could optimize by using `np.fft.rfft(p, n=N2)`? Need p_rev. Fine.

In funjac, we can compute p and y and load. But for objective only, L-BFGS may call scalar? With jac=True, returns tuple; no separate scalar.

```
def _make_optimizer(p, n):
    conv=_make_convolver(n)
    load=_make_loader(n)
    L=2*n-1
    M0 = max(conv(p))
    y0=conv(p); u0=np.zeros(L); idx=y0==M0; u0[idx]=1/idx.sum(); load0=load(p,u0)
    l=load0 - np.dot(p,load0)
    grad0 = p*l
    norm0=np.linalg.norm(grad0[1:])
    C = 1.0 / max(norm0, 1e-12)
    C = min(C, 1e10)
    logL=np.log(L)
    def funtheta(theta):
       allt = np.empty(n); allt[0]=0; allt[1:]=theta
       allt -= allt.max()
       exp = np.exp(np.clip(allt, -745, 745))
       p = exp; p /= p.sum()
       y = conv(p)
       M = y.max()
       # alpha from closure
       if alpha <= 0: ...
       e = np.exp(np.clip((y-M)/alpha, -745, 0.0))
       Z = e.sum()
       val = C*(M + alpha*(np.log(Z) - logL))
       # if val nan, return large
       if not np.isfinite(val): return (1e100, None?)
       u = e/Z
       loadl = load(p,u)
       l = C*loadl
       dot = np.dot(p,l)
       grad = p*(l-dot)
       grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       # ensure reduced gradient
       return val, grad[1:].copy()
    return funtheta, C
```

    p_new=softmax from theta
    if valid and finite: score=evaluate; if finite update

def active_loop(p, budget):
   start=time.perf_counter()
   end=start+budget
   p=p.copy(); current_est=2n*conv(p).max()
   no_improve=0
   eta=0.1

      if time.monotonic()>deadline-5 or time.perf_counter()>end: break
      y=conv(p); M=y.max(); est=2n*M

      u=np.zeros(L); u[act]=1/act.sum()

      l=loadl - loadl.mean()
      sd=np.sqrt(np.mean(l*l)); if sd<1e-12: break
      l /= sd
      accepted=False
      for e in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.2, eta*0.5?):
          p2=p*np.exp(-e*l)
          p2=np.maximum(p2,0); s=p2.sum(); if s<=0: continue
          p2/=s
          est2=2*n*conv(p2).max()
          if est2 < est - 1e-12:
             p=p2; current_est=est2; eta=e; accepted=True; break
      if accepted:
          no_improve=0
          if top_frac>0.002: top_frac*=0.95; eta*=0.98
          if it%50==0: exact update
      else:
          no_improve+=1
          eta*=0.7

             no_improve=0

          elif no_improve>50: break
   return p

Let's verify the load formula with an example to avoid off-by-one. For p length2 [a,b]. y=[a^2,2ab,b^2] L=3. u=[u0,u1,u2]. load_j =2∑_{i=0}^{1} p_i u_{i+j}.

m0=b u0
m1=b u1 + a u0
m2=b u2 + a u1
m3=a u2
n-1=1, t[0]=m1=b u1+a u0, t[1]=m2=b u2+a u1. load=2t. Good.

Let's define `make_objective(p, alpha, C=None)` and compute C outside.

```
def compute_scale(p, alpha):
    y=conv(p); M=y.max()
    z=np.clip((y-M)/alpha, -745, 0); e=np.exp(z); Z=e.sum(); u=e/Z
    l=load(p,u)
    dot=np.dot(p,l)
    grad=p*(l-dot)
    norm=np.linalg.norm(grad[1:])
    if norm < 1e-12: return None
    C = 1.0 / norm
    if C > 1e10: C=1e10
    if C < 1e-6: C=1e-6
    return C
```
Using C=1/norm means gradient norm ~1. But the objective value C*m may be enormous if C=1e10? m~1e-4 =>1e6, okay. If norm tiny, maybe stop.

Pseudo active loop:
```
def active_loop(p, budget_s):
    end = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    p=p.copy(); p/=p.sum()
    cur_est = 2*n*conv(p).max()
    eta=0.1
    top_frac=0.02
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < end and no < 30:
        y=conv(p); M=y.max(); est=2*n*M
        if M<=0: break
        kmax = max(5, int(0.005*L)) # top 0.5%
        thresh = M - top_frac*M
        act = y >= thresh
        if act.sum() < kmax:
            kk=min(L, max(kmax, int(0.002*L)))
            idx = np.argpartition(y, -kk)[-kk:] if kk<L else np.arange(L)
            act = np.zeros(L, bool); act[idx]=True
        u=np.zeros(L); u[act]=1/act.sum()
        loadl=load(p,u)
        l=loadl - loadl.mean()
        # Use quantile scale
        q99=np.percentile(np.abs(l), 99)
        if q99<1e-12: break
        l/=q99
        accepted=False
        for e in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1):
            p2=p*np.exp(-e*l)
            p2=np.maximum(p2,0)
            s=p2.sum()
            if s<=0: continue
            p2/=s
            est2=2*n*conv(p2).max()
            if est2 < est - 1e-13:
                p=p2; eta=e; accepted=True; break
        if accepted:
            no=0
            if top_frac > 0.002: top_frac *=0.97
            if time time: update_best exact if ...
        else:
            no +=1
            eta = max(0.005, eta*0.6)
            top_frac = min(0.05, top_frac*1.25)
            if no >= 8:
                # perturb
                p = p * np.exp(0.03*rng.normal(size=n)); p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
                no=0
    return p
```

Implementation of `run_minimize`:
```
def run_minimize(start_p, alpha, quota):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: return start_p
    start_p=np.asarray(start_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    start_p=np.maximum(start_p,0.0); s=start_p.sum()
    if s<=0: return best_p.copy()
    start_p/=s; start_p=np.minimum(start_p,1000.0); start_p/=start_p.sum()
    # compute scale
    y0=conv_self(start_p); M0=y0.max()
    if not finite M0 or M0<=0: return start_p
    z=np.clip((y0-M0)/alpha, -745.0, 0.0)
    e0=np.exp(z); Z0=e0.sum(); if Z0<=0: return start_p
    u0=e0/Z0
    l0=load_from(start_p,u0)
    dot0=float(np.dot(start_p,l0))
    grad0=start_p*(l0-dot0)
    norm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad0[1:])) if n>1 else 0
    if not finite norm or norm<1e-14: return start_p
    C=1.0/norm
    C=float(min(1e10, max(1e-3, C)))
    logL=np.log(float(L))
    def fun(theta): ...
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(start_p,1e-300)); theta -= theta[0]
    # benchmark
    tb=time.perf_counter(); val,gr=fun(theta); tf=time.perf_counter()-tb
    if not finite val: return start_p
    if tf <= 0: tf=1e-3
    maxfun=int(min(1500, max(30, int(0.5*quota / tf))))
    if maxfun < 10: return start_p
    options={'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':maxfun,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':30}
    try:
       res=minimize(fun, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
           p2=theta_to_p(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float))
       else: p2=start_p
    except Exception:
       p2=start_p
    p2=np.maximum(p2,0); s=p2.sum(); if s>0: p2/=s
    return p2
```
`C` max 1e10. If norm huge, C small; maybe set min 1e-6.

```
def theta_to_p(theta):
    if len(theta)!=n-1: ...
    allt=np.empty(n, dtype=float); allt[0]=0; allt[1:]=theta
    allt=nan_to_num(allt, nan=0, posinf=50, neginf=-50)
    allt -= allt.max()
    np.clip(allt, -745, 0, out=allt)
    e=np.exp(allt); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    return e/s
```

In active loop, use `conv_self` and `load_from`; update best with `update_best` but not too often.
```
def active_loop(start_p, budget_s):
   p=np.asarray(start_p).copy(); p/=p.sum()
   end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
   eta=0.08; top_frac=0.02; no=0; last_exact=best_score
   while time.monotonic() < end and no<40:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline-8: break
      y=conv_self(p); M=y.max(); est=2*n*M
      if not finite M: break
      kmin=max(8, int(0.002*L))
      act = y >= (M - top_frac*M)
      if act.sum() < kmin:
         k=min(L, max(kmin,5))
         idx=np.argpartition(y, L-k)[-k:] if k<L else np.arange(L)
         act=np.zeros(L, bool); act[idx]=True
      u=np.zeros(L); u[act]=1.0/act.sum()
      l=load_from(p,u)
      l -= l.mean()
      rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(l*l))
      if rms<1e-12: break
      l /= rms
      l = np.clip(l, -50, 50)
      accepted=False
      for e in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1):
         p2=p*np.exp(-e*l)
         p2=np.maximum(p2,0); s=p2.sum()
         if s<=0: continue
         p2/=s
         est2=2*n*conv_self(p2).max()
         if est2 < est - 1e-13:
             p=p2; eta=e; accepted=True; break
      if accepted:
         no=0
         if top_frac>0.002: top_frac*=0.97
         if est < last_exact - 1e-6 or no...:
             update_best(p); last_exact=best_score
      else:
         no +=1
         eta=max(0.005, eta*0.7)
         top_frac=min(0.05, top_frac*1.3)
         if no % 6 == 0:
             # small perturbation
             p = p * np.exp(0.03*rng.normal(size=n))
             p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s>0:p/=s
             no=0 #? maybe keep no? To avoid infinite.
          if no>30: break
   return p
```
If perturbation resets no, loop may run until time budget. Fine.

But `np.argpartition(y, L-k)` with k maybe L? If k<L. For L large and kmin maybe 18, good. If y has nan? no.

Now, one more mathematical subtlety: The evaluator's convolution uses `np.convolve` of sequence with itself. Our FFT conv uses rfft squared and `irfft` with linear zero-padding. For real p, `irfft(F*F)` yields circular convolution length N. Since N >= 2n-1, the first 2n-1 are linear convolution. Good. Small negative clipped to 0? If we clip negative y to 0 in `conv_self`, this changes y for maximum? But max positive; only tiny negatives. Could if numerical underflow makes a positive coefficient slightly negative? Not likely for max. Clipping all negatives to 0 is safe. Use `np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)`? This may allocate. Use `y = np.where(y < 0.0, 0.0, y)`. Small.

In load, `u` nonnegative; t positive? load positive. No clip.

Let's now write final code. Ensure final answer only code block.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    n = 1
    m = int(max(1, m))
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial construction.  Use the supplied parent when available.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        n_fallback = 4498
        q = np.empty(n_fallback, dtype=np.float64)
        q[0] = 1.0
        for i in range(n_fallback - 1):
            q[i + 1] = q[i] * (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * i + 2.0)
        arr = q[::-1]

    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return [1.0]

    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-9:
        arr = np.ones_like(arr)
        s = float(arr.size)
    p0 = arr / s

    n = int(arr.size)
    if n == 1:
        return [1.0]

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N_conv = _next_pow2(L)
    N_load = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    logL = float(np.log(float(L)))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fast FFT primitives for the exact score structure.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def conv_self(p: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        F = np.fft.rfft(p, N_conv)
        y = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N_conv)[:L]
        return np.where(y < 0.0, 0.0, y)

    def load_au(p: np.ndarray, u: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        # load_i = 2 * sum_j p_j u_{i+j},  np.convolve implements linear conv.
        pr = p[::-1]
        Fpr = np.fft.rfft(pr, N_load)
        Fu = np.fft.rfft(u, N_load)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Fpr * Fu, N_load)
        return 2.0 * c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    def theta_to_p(theta: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
        if theta.size != n - 1:
            throw_tail = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            theta = throw_tail
        allt = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        allt[0] = 0.0
        allt[1:] = theta
        finite_mask = np.isfinite(allt)
        if not finite_mask.all():
            allt = np.where(finite_mask, allt, 0.0)
        allt = np.nan_to_num(allt, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
        allt -= float(np.max(allt))
        np.clip(allt, -745.0, 0.0, out=allt)
        e = np.exp(allt)
        ss = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        out = e / ss
        return out

    def fft_score(p: np.ndarray) -> float:
        y = conv_self(p)
        return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(y))))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Best-so-far tracking with the authoritative evaluator.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p]))

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p]))

    def update_best(p: np.ndarray) -> None:
        nonlocal best_p, best_score
        q = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if q.size != n:
            return
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ss = float(np.sum(q))
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss < 0.01:
            return
        q = q / ss
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in q]))
        except Exception:
            return
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = q.copy()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # L-BFGS polish on the normalized simplex, using a smoothed maximum.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def run_l_bfgs(start_p: np.ndarray, alpha: float, quota_s: float) -> np.ndarray:
        if _scipy_minimize is None:
            return start_p
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
            return start_p

        start_p = np.asarray(start_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        start_p = np.clip(start_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ss = float(np.sum(start_p))
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            return best_p.copy()
        start_p = start_p / ss

        alpha = max(1e-9, float(alpha))

        # Adaptive constant so the initial reduced gradient has norm near 1.
        y0 = conv_self(start_p)
        M0 = float(np.max(y0))
        if not np.isfinite(M0) or M0 <= 0.0:
            return start_p

        z0 = np.clip((y0 - M0) / alpha, -745.0, 0.0)
        e0 = np.exp(z0)
        Z0 = float(np.sum(e0))
        if not np.isfinite(Z0) or Z0 <= 0.0:
            return start_p
        u0 = e0 / Z0

        l0 = load_au(start_p, u0)
        dot0 = float(np.dot(start_p, l0))
        g0 = start_p * (l0 - dot0)
        norm0 = float(np.linalg.norm(g0[1:]))
        if not np.isfinite(norm0) or norm0 < 1e-14:
            return start_p

        C = 1.0 / norm0
        if not np.isfinite(C):
            C = 1e6
        C = float(min(1e10, max(1e-6, C)))

        def fun_theta(theta):
            theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
            if theta.size != n - 1 or (theta.size and not np.all(np.isfinite(theta))):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

            allt = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            allt[0] = 0.0
            allt[1:] = theta
            allt = np.nan_to_num(allt, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
            allt -= float(np.max(allt))
            np.clip(allt, -745.0, 0.0, out=allt)
            ep = np.exp(allt)
            sp = float(np.sum(ep))
            if not np.isfinite(sp) or sp <= 0.0:
                return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            p = ep / sp

            y = conv_self(p)
            M = float(np.max(y))
            if not np.isfinite(M):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

            zz = np.clip((y - M) / alpha, -745.0, 0.0)
            ee = np.exp(zz)
            ZZ = float(np.sum(ee))
            if not np.isfinite(ZZ) or ZZ <= 0.0:
                ZZ = 1.0
                ee = np.zeros_like(y)
                idxM = int(np.argmax(y))
                ee[idxM] = 1.0

            val = C * (M + alpha * (float(np.log(ZZ)) - logL))
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

            u = ee / ZZ
            l = C * load_au(p, u)
            l = np.nan_to_num(l, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            dot = float(np.dot(p, l))
            grad_full = p * (l - dot)
            grad = grad_full[1:].copy()
            grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            return float(val), grad

        p_log = start_p + 1e-12
        p_log = np.maximum(p_log, 1e-300)
        p_log = p_log / float(np.sum(p_log))
        theta0 = np.log(p_log)
        theta0 -= float(theta0[0])
        theta0 = np.clip(theta0, -100.0, 100.0)

        try:
            t_bench = time.perf_counter()
            val0, grad0b = fun_theta(theta0)
            tf = time.perf_counter() - t_bench
        except Exception:
            return start_p

        if not np.isfinite(val0):
            return start_p
        if tf <= 0.0:
            tf = 1e-3

        maxfun = int(min(2000, max(30, int(0.55 * max(1.0, quota_s) / max(tf, 1e-3)))))
        options = {
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxiter": maxfun,
            "ftol": 1e-10,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "maxls": 30,
        }

        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                fun_theta,
                theta0,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options=options,
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                out = theta_to_p(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
            else:
                out = start_p.copy()
        except Exception:
            out = start_p.copy()

        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ss = float(np.sum(out))
        if np.isfinite(ss) and ss > 0.0:
            out = out / ss
        else:
            out = start_p.copy()
        return out

    def perturb(p: np.ndarray, scale: float) -> np.ndarray:
        q = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        q *= np.exp(scale * rng.normal(size=n))
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        ss = float(np.sum(q))
        if np.isfinite(ss) and ss > 0.0:
            q = q / ss
        else:
            q = best_p.copy()
        return q

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main optimization schedule.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        alphas = [2.0e-7, 1.0e-7, 5.0e-8, 2.5e-8, 1.2e-8, 6.0e-9]
        perturb_count = 0
        last_improve_score = best_score

        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160.0:
                break

            before = best_score
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining <= 120.0:
                break

            quota = min(180.0, max(60.0, 0.45 * remaining))

            if perturb_count == 0:
                start = best_p.copy()
            elif perturb_count == 1:
                start = perturb(best_p, 0.045)
            else:
                start = perturb(best_p, 0.08)

                p_new = run_l_bfgs(start, alpha, quota)
                update_best(p_new)
                if best_score < before - 1e-13:
                    perturb_count = 0
                    last_improve_score = best_score
                    continue
                break

            p_new = run_l_bfgs(start, alpha, quota)
            update_best(p_new)

            if best_score < before - 1e-13:
                perturb_count = 0
                last_improve_score = best_score
            else:
                # Freeze deterministic improvements; try a slightly different basin next.
                perturb_count = min(perturb_count + 1, 2)
                if perturb_count > 2:
                    break

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final active-set mirror polish.  This uses only monotonically
    # improving exact/FFT-estimated moves and is safe as a fallback.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def active_polish(start_p: np.ndarray, budget_s: float) -> np.ndarray:
        p = np.asarray(start_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ss = float(np.sum(p))
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            return best_p.copy()
        p = p / ss

        end_time = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s)
        eta = 0.07
        top_frac = 0.018
        no_improve = 0
        steps_since_exact = 0
        kmin = max(8, int(0.002 * L))

        while time.monotonic() < end_time and time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
            y = conv_self(p)
            M = float(np.max(y))
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                break
            est = 2.0 * n * M

            act = y >= (M - top_frac * M)
            if int(np.sum(act)) < kmin:
                k = min(L, max(8, kmin))
                if k < L:
                    idx = np.argpartition(y, L - k)[-k:]
                else:
                    idx = np.arange(L)
                act = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
                act[idx] = True

            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[act] = 1.0 / float(np.sum(act))
            l = load_au(p, u)
            l -= float(np.mean(l))
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(l * l)))
            if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-14:
                break
            l = np.clip(l / rms, -50.0, 50.0)

            accepted = False
            for e in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta, 0.1 * eta):
                if e <= 0.0:
                    continue
                q = p * np.exp(-e * l)
                q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
                sq = float(np.sum(q))
                if not np.isfinite(sq) or sq <= 0.0:
                    continue
                q = q / sq
                est2 = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(conv_self(q)))
                if np.isfinite(est2) and est2 < est - 1e-11:
                    p = q
                    eta = e
                    accepted = True
                    break

            if accepted:
                no_improve = 0
                steps_since_exact += 1
                if top_frac > 0.002:
                    top_frac *= 0.97
                if est < best_score - 1e-6 or steps_since_exact >= 40:
                    update_best(p)
                    steps_since_exact = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                eta = max(0.004, 0.7 * eta)
                top_frac = min(0.05, 1.35 * top_frac)
                if no_improve > 24:
                    break
                if no_improve % 6 == 0:
                    p = perturb(p, 0.03)

        return p

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        budget_active = min(420.0, max(0.0, remaining - 50.0))
        if budget_active > 60.0:
            p_polished = active_polish(best_p.copy(), budget_active)
            update_best(p_polished)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final authorization and fallback.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    result = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))

    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score > 2.0 + 1e-12:
        fb = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        result = [float(x) for x in fb]
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))

    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
        return result

    # A tiny final cleanup: normalize to sum one exactly and re-evaluate.
    arr = np.asarray(result, dtype=np.float64)
    ss = float(np.sum(arr))
    if np.isfinite(ss) and ss > 0.0:
        arr = arr / ss
        clean = [float(x) for x in arr]
        clean_score = float(evaluate_sequence(clean))
        if np.isfinite(clean_score) and clean_score < final_score:
            result = clean
            final_score = clean_score

    return result
```
